# Graph Report - floem  (2026-10-25)

## Corpus Check
- 2272 files · ~2,119,131 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 37902 nodes · 85187 edges · 1433 communities (1276 shown, 65 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1268 edges (avg confidence: 0.85)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `79d87111`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
